# FS-Billing verification map

This directory is the maintained source for verifying FS-Billing's user-facing behavior. It covers the customer portal and admin shell in `apps/web`, the `/api` surface of `apps/api`, the auth CLIs, and the separate FreeSWITCH/SIPp e2e. Read this index before driving, then use the matching feature file as the recipe. `S=.cursor/skills/verify-fs-billing/scripts` throughout.

## Baseline preconditions

- A run launched by `$S/launch.sh`, with its own `postgres:17.9-alpine` container `fsb-verify-<RUN_ID>`, its own API and web ports, and an empty database that was migrated, seeded, and given credentials by `auth:seed`.
- `$S/doctor.sh` prints `doctor: healthy` for that run. Never drive a stack the run did not start. Never touch `fs-billing-postgres-1` or `127.0.0.1:5432`.
- Seed facts the journeys rely on (`packages/db/src/seed.ts`):
  - ABC001 is a CNY tenant with balance `-35.20`.
  - GLB009 is a USD tenant with balance `-15.02`.
  - CDT018 is `disabled`.
  - The admins are `wangmin@example.com` (王敏) and `lijun@example.com` (李俊).
  - Every portal login is `<lowercase code>@example.com`, and every seeded account uses `SEED_AUTH_PASSWORD`.
- No seeded account has a live lock in `auth.login_failures`. Every journey checks this and exits BLOCKED if one does.

## Driving conventions

- Browser actions go through `$S/drive.mjs <feature-id>`, a headless Playwright Chromium against the run's web origin. Use `--headed` to watch.
- HTTP actions go through `$S/api.sh`, via the web origin's `/api` proxy with the web `Origin` header.
- DB reads go through `$S/sql.sh "<sql>"`, as the owner role in the run's container. Repo CLIs go through `$S/with-env.sh <command>`.
- Use ARIA roles and accessible names from `apps/web/src`, quoted exactly as in the feature files (Chinese copy included). Do not use CSS classes or positions.
- Journeys create sessions and failure counters as they run. Each one leaves the accounts it used unlocked. Run features one at a time, or all of them with `$S/drive.mjs all`. Login and change-password share a 30-per-minute-per-IP rate limit, so each journey first waits until 61 s have passed since the run's last credential request.
- A fresh launch is the reset. If a run's state is unclear, clean it up and launch again. Do not hand-edit rows to restore a baseline.

## Proof and skip reporting

- Every browser step records the user action, the HTTP status and error `code` behind it, and the screen before and after as `.png` and `.aria.yml`. API-only steps record `.http` transcripts instead.
- Every state change is also checked in rows: `auth.session`, `auth.login_failures`, `auth.account`, `operation_logs`, `tenant_balances`. The query and its rows are saved to `.sql.txt`.
- A DB edit that stands in for elapsed time or an operator action is saved as its own `.sql.txt` and named in the step (expiring `auth.session.expires_at`, deleting session rows).
- Evidence goes to `docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/<feature-id>/`. `steps.md` and `result.json` give PASS, FAIL, or BLOCKED per step.
- BLOCKED (exit 2) means a precondition was missing. Report it with the reason, and never as PASS. Do not report an entry point as verified through a different entry point.
- Report the run's git revision from `doctor.sh`, and note any uncommitted paths.

## Feature entry contract

Each feature file starts with an H1 title and one paragraph describing the user-visible behavior. It then uses exactly four H2 sections, in this order:

1. `Sub-features`: short IDs, one line per behavior. They match the step IDs in `drive.mjs` output.
2. `How to get to it (user POV)`: every user entry point.
3. `Driving it with <harness>`: starts with `Preconditions:`, then labeled bullets. Each bullet pairs a user action with the exact command and the observable result, including DB side effects.
4. `Gotchas`: traps that can waste or invalidate a run.

Keep implementation details out of the map. Name only user paths, stable handles, required state, commands, and observable proof.

## Features

- [customer-login](./customer-login.md): portal login, redirect when signed out, the balance on the home page, and the session row.
- [admin-login](./admin-login.md): admin login into the admin shell. The account kind decides the zone, whichever login page was used.
- [login-errors](./login-errors.md): one message for an unknown email and a wrong password, lockout after 5 failures with a countdown, emergency unlock, and the disabled account.
- [session-expiry](./session-expiry.md): an expired or revoked session goes to login, then back to the original URL with its query.
- [cross-tenant](./cross-tenant.md): tenant A cannot read B, customer and admin APIs are split, and the IDOR suite.
- [logout](./logout.md): logout from the desktop menu and the mobile sheet removes the session row, and revisiting redirects to login.
- [theme-and-mobile](./theme-and-mobile.md): light, dark, and system themes persist; the 375px layout; the account sheet and nav drawer.
- [not-found](./not-found.md): the 404 page in the portal and admin zones, signed in and signed out, plus the API 404.
- [freeswitch-sipp](./freeswitch-sipp.md): a separate surface. How to run the SIPp e2e, what PASS looks like, and why it is not isolated.
# Admin login

An internal admin signs in on `/admin/login` and lands in the admin shell at `/admin`: the `概览` page, the admin's name in the account menu, and the admin accent (`html[data-product=admin]`). Where you land depends on the account kind in the login response, not on the login page used. A customer who signs in on the admin login page lands in the portal, and an admin who signs in on the portal login page lands in the admin shell.

## Sub-features

- `entry-redirect`: a signed-out visit to `/admin` redirects to `/admin/login`, which shows `heading '登录管理后台'` and the hint `客户请使用客户门户登录`.
- `admin-shell`: `wangmin@example.com` reaches `/admin` with `heading '概览'`, `button '账号菜单'` containing `王敏`, and the text `欢迎，王敏。…`. An `auth.session` row joined to `admins` exists.
- `kind-decides-zone`: a customer logging in on `/admin/login` lands on `/`, and a later visit to `/admin` bounces to `/`.
- `admin-via-portal-login`: an admin logging in on `/login` lands on `/admin`.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- Open `/admin/login` directly.
- Open any `/admin…` URL while signed out. The app redirects to `/admin/login`.
- Log in as an admin on the portal page `/login`. The app routes by account kind.

## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy.
- `wangmin@example.com` and `abc001@example.com` are not locked.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs admin-login`. Exit `0`, and `steps.md` lists the four sub-feature IDs as PASS.
- **Signed-out redirect.** Open `/admin`. The URL becomes `/admin/login`, and the page shows `heading '登录管理后台'` and `客户请使用客户门户登录`. Evidence: `01-admin-login.png`, `.aria.yml`.
- **Admin login.** On `/admin/login`, fill `登录邮箱` with `wangmin@example.com` and the seeded password, then click `登录`. The URL becomes `/admin`, and the page shows `heading '概览'`, `欢迎，王敏。`, `button '账号菜单'` with `王敏`, and `html[data-product=admin]`. Evidence: `02-admin-home.png`, `.aria.yml`. `02-admin-session.sql.txt` shows `wangmin@example.com | 王敏 | t`.
- **Customer on the admin page.** In a fresh context, log in on `/admin/login` as `abc001@example.com`. The URL becomes `/` and `heading '首页'` is visible. Then `page.goto('/admin')` stays on `/`. Evidence: `03-customer-via-admin-login.png`.
- **Admin on the portal page.** In a fresh context, log in on `/login` as `wangmin@example.com`. The URL becomes `/admin`.
- **API view.** Run `$S/api.sh login wangmin@example.com adm` then `$S/api.sh GET /api/admin/context adm`. The response is 200 with `"identity":{"kind":"admin",…,"name":"王敏"}`.

## Gotchas

- `/admin/login` shows `登录管理后台` as the h1 on both desktop and mobile. The mobile layout also shows an `FS-Billing 管理后台` pill. Do not assert the portal copy here.
- Routing by kind happens client-side after a 200 login. The API accepts a customer login on either page, so the API response alone never proves the zone. Assert the final URL.
- Admin login creates a normal 7-day session, and nothing in this journey logs it out. Later journeys that count `wangmin` sessions must count before and after rather than expect zero.
# Cross-tenant isolation

A customer can read only their own tenant's data, and customer and admin APIs are kept apart. The portal API takes no tenant or account ID from the client: the tenant comes from the session. So customer A's cookie always returns A's data, and B's IDs in admin URLs are refused with 403. A customer cookie on admin endpoints and an admin cookie on portal endpoints both get `403 FORBIDDEN`, and no cookie gets `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`. The repo's IDOR suite enumerates every customer, authenticated, and session operation from the live OpenAPI and checks both defenses: explicit filters and RLS.

## Sub-features

- `own-tenant-only`: A (`abc001`) and B (`glb009`) each `GET /api/portal/context` and each body equals, field for field, the only context that customer may see: its own profile, email, tenant (id, code, name, currency from the DB) and balance (A `-35.20 CNY`, B `-15.02 USD`). Any extra field fails the step.
- `customer-to-admin`: A's cookie on `GET /api/admin/context` and on `POST /api/admin/portal-accounts/<B's id>/reset-password` gets `403 FORBIDDEN`. B's `auth.account.updated_at` is unchanged and `operation_logs` has 0 `reset_password` rows.
- `admin-to-portal`: an admin cookie on `GET /api/portal/context` gets `403 FORBIDDEN`.
- `anonymous`: no cookie gets `401 UNAUTHENTICATED` with `action: reauthenticate`.
- `idor-suite`: `pnpm test:idor` reports `Tests 15 passed (15)`, run on a scratch `fs_billing_test` DB inside the run's container.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- A logged-in customer's browser calls `/api/portal/context` from the portal home. Any HTTP client holding their cookie can call any `/api/*` path.
- A customer pastes an admin URL such as `/admin` into the browser. The UI bounces them to `/` (see [admin-login](./admin-login.md)), and this feature proves the API also refuses.
- A developer runs `pnpm test:idor`, and CI runs it too.

## Driving it with curl via api.sh (drive.mjs orchestrates)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001`, `glb009`, and `wangmin` are not locked.
- `operation_logs` has no `reset_password` rows. A fresh launch has none.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs cross-tenant`. Exit `0`, five steps PASS. Transcripts are saved as `NN-*.http`.
- **Two tenants.** Run `$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com a`, `$S/api.sh login glb009@example.com b`, `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context a`, and `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context b`. Jar `a` returns `"code":"ABC001"` and `"amount":"-35.20"`. Jar `b` returns `"code":"GLB009"` and `"amount":"-15.02"`. The journey compares each whole body with one built from the DB for that customer, so an extra key or foreign tenant data fails. `05-tenant-ids.sql.txt` maps codes to ids and balances.
- **Customer on admin API.** Run `$S/api.sh GET /api/admin/context a`. The response is `403` with `"code":"FORBIDDEN"`. Get B's portal account id with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT p.id FROM portal_accounts p JOIN tenants t ON t.id=p.tenant_id WHERE t.code='GLB009'"`, then run `$S/api.sh POST /api/admin/portal-accounts/<id>/reset-password a`. The response is `403 FORBIDDEN`. `08-b-credential-before-after.sql.txt` records B's `auth.account.updated_at` before and after the attempt, and the step fails unless they are equal. `08-no-reset-side-effect.sql.txt` shows the row and `reset_logs 0`.
- **Admin on portal API.** Run `$S/api.sh login wangmin@example.com admin` then `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context admin`. The response is `403 FORBIDDEN`.
- **Anonymous.** Run `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context`. The response is `401` with `"code":"UNAUTHENTICATED"` and `"action":"reauthenticate"`.
- **IDOR suite.** Run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm test:idor`. The output contains `Tests  15 passed (15)` (`13-test-idor.cli.txt`). The suite creates and drops `fs_billing_test` in OUR container. Confirm with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT datname FROM pg_database"`: no `fs_billing_test` remains.
- **Mutation proof (optional, slower).** Run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api prove:idor docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/cross-tenant/prove-idor`. It must report that baseline-app and baseline-owner pass, that mutation-owner detects B's balance leak, and that mutation-app passes (`docs/phase-4-api.md`).

## Gotchas

- There is no portal endpoint that takes a tenant id, so "A reads B by changing an id" cannot be attempted on the portal API. The real attack surfaces are the admin endpoints that take ids (the reset-password routes) and session or cookie confusion. Do not invent a URL like `/api/portal/tenants/<B>`: it returns `404 NOT_FOUND`, which proves nothing.
- `test:idor` and `prove:idor` load the root `.env`. Run them only through `with-env.sh`, or they drop and recreate `fs_billing_test` on the user's `:5432` server.
- Do not run `test:idor` while another DB suite runs against the same container. It drops and recreates `fs_billing_test` every time.
- POSTs need `Origin` set to a trusted origin, or they return `403 FORBIDDEN` before authorization runs. `api.sh` sends the web origin. A hand-written curl without it gives a misleading 403.
# Customer login

A customer signs in on the portal login page with their login email and password and lands on the portal home (`/`). The home page shows their tenant code and name in the top bar and the current balance in the tenant's currency. A signed-out visit to any portal page redirects to `/login`.

## Sub-features

- `entry-redirect`: a signed-out visit to `/` redirects to `/login`, which shows the `登录` form.
- `portal-login`: valid credentials return 200 with `identity.kind = customer` and navigate to `/`.
- `balance`: the home page shows the crumb `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司` and the region `当前余额` with `-¥35.20` and `CNY`.
- `db-side-effects`: an `auth.session` row with a 7-day TTL exists, and the DB balance equals the UI balance.
- `usd-balance`: a USD tenant sees a dollar balance (`-$15.02`).

## How to get to it (user POV)

- Open the web origin's `/login` directly.
- Open any portal URL (`/`, `/?tab=…`) while signed out. The app redirects to `/login`.
- Use `/admin/login` with a customer account. See [admin-login](./admin-login.md) `kind-decides-zone`; it also ends on `/`.

## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy.
- `abc001@example.com` and `glb009@example.com` are not locked. The journey checks this and exits 2 if either is locked.
- The seed is unchanged: ABC001 has `-35.20` CNY and GLB009 has `-15.02` USD.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs customer-login`. Exit `0` and every step shows PASS in `docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/customer-login/steps.md`.
- **Signed-out redirect.** Open `/`. Step `entry-redirect`. The URL becomes `/login` and `heading '登录'` is visible. Evidence: `01-login-form.png`, `01-login-form.aria.yml`.
- **Log in.** Fill `登录邮箱` with `abc001@example.com` and `密码` with the seeded password, then click `button '登录'`. Step `portal-login`. `browser-http.txt` shows `POST /api/auth/login -> 200` with `"kind":"customer"` and `"code":"ABC001"`, and the URL becomes `/`.
- **Balance shown.** Step `balance`. The page shows `heading '首页'`, the crumb `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司`, and `region '当前余额'` containing `-¥35.20` and `CNY`, with `html[data-product=portal]`. Evidence: `02-portal-home.png`, `02-portal-home.aria.yml`.
- **Session row and DB balance.** Step `db-side-effects` reads them with `$S/sql.sh`. `03-session-and-balance.sql.txt` shows `abc001@example.com | t | 127.0.0.1 | -35.20`: a session expiring more than 6 days out, and `tenant_balances` equal to the UI value.
- **USD tenant.** Log in as `glb009@example.com` in a fresh context. Step `usd-balance`. `region '当前余额'` contains `-$15.02`. Evidence: `04-usd-home.png`.
- **API-only check.** Run `$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com a` then `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context a`. The response is 200 with `"balance":{"currency":"CNY","amount":"-35.20"}`.

## Gotchas

- The desktop form has the visible `heading '登录'`. Below 640px that h1 is hidden and `登录客户门户` shows instead, so use a desktop viewport for this journey.
- The balance renders only after `/api/portal/context` resolves, and a skeleton shows until then. Assert on the region text, not on the skeleton being gone.
- `-¥35.20` is red because the balance is negative. Any change to the seed balance changes the expected string. Fix the seed expectation in this file and in `drive.mjs` together, and never loosen it to a regex.
- A successful login also deletes that email's `auth.login_failures` row. Logging in before [login-errors](./login-errors.md) can hide a counter you meant to inspect.
- `bjh003@example.com` belongs to the launch ready check. Do not use it in journeys.
# FreeSWITCH / SIPp e2e (separate surface)

The telephony surface has no UI. A softswitch user places a call through FreeSWITCH. The billing Lua asks the Worker to authorize it (`maxSeconds`), FreeSWITCH bridges or rejects the call (402/403/484), and on hangup posts the CDR through the nginx relay to the Worker, which inserts `raw_cdrs`/`cdrs` and debits the ledger. `freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh` drives this end to end with real FreeSWITCH, SIPp, nginx, and Worker processes and an observer that captures INVITEs. The browser harness (`drive.mjs`) does not drive it, and `launch.sh` does not start it.

## Sub-features

- `number-cases`: six dialing formats (`+44`, `0044`, domestic mobile, domestic landline, `+1305`, bare `1305`). In each, the upstream INVITE keeps the original number. The authorize request has `callee == billingNumber` normalized and `dialedNumber` raw. The CDR `billing_number` is normalized.
- `rejects`: a disabled tenant gets SIP 403, insufficient balance gets 402, and an invalid number gets 484 before any authorize request. None is bridged upstream.
- `degrade`: when the Worker refuses or stalls, the call degrades per the Lua rules, and the observer records no authorize request.
- `time-limit`: a real 60 s allotted call lasts 59–60 s. It is billed 1 minute, CDR amount `0.01`, ledger `-0.01`, balance `0.01 → 0`.
- `cdr-replay`: with nginx down, the CDR lands in the retry dir. `replay-cdr.sh` re-posts it after nginx returns, and exactly one charge results.
- `lua-unit`: `lua5.3 freeswitch/tests/billing_authorize_test.lua` (also run by `pnpm check`).

## How to get to it (user POV)

- CI: `.github/workflows/freeswitch.yml` job `e2e`, on push or PR touching `freeswitch/**`, `apps/worker/**`, or `packages/**`, nightly, or `workflow_dispatch`. It needs the private GHCR image built by the `image` job (`SIGNALWIRE_TOKEN`).
- Locally: `bash freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh [log-dir]`, with the images `fs-billing-freeswitch:local` and the SIPp image built from `freeswitch/test/sipp.Dockerfile`. `--build` rebuilds FreeSWITCH and needs `SIGNALWIRE_TOKEN`.
- A real test-bench call (phase 10) is a different acceptance, outside this skill.

## Driving it with run-integration.sh

Preconditions:

- Linux with Docker host networking, Node, pnpm, Python 3, curl, and `docker images` showing `fs-billing-freeswitch:local` (or set `FS_IMAGE`).
- Host ports `3001` (Worker), `3002` (nginx relay), `3003` (stall), `3004` (authorize observer), and `5060`/`5062` (SIP) are free. They are fixed and host-networked, so only one SIPp run per machine, and never alongside a dev Worker on 3001.
- **Isolation.** By default the script runs `docker compose -f compose.yaml` against the user's compose project, which is `fs-billing-postgres-1` on `:5432`, migrates it, inserts `FSI…` test tenants, and stops it afterwards if it started it. Never run it bare here. Point it at a throwaway compose project instead:

  ```sh
  SIPP_RUN=fsb-sipp-$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)
  SIPP_PORT=$(python3 -c 'import socket; s=socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1",0)); print(s.getsockname()[1])')
  SIPP_PW=$(python3 -c 'import secrets; print(secrets.token_hex(16))')
  SIPP_LOG=docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/$SIPP_RUN/freeswitch-sipp
  COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=$SIPP_RUN POSTGRES_PORT=$SIPP_PORT POSTGRES_PASSWORD=$SIPP_PW \
  DATABASE_URL=postgresql://fs_billing:$SIPP_PW@127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT/fs_billing \
  APP_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://fs_billing_app:$SIPP_PW-app@127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT/fs_billing \
    bash freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh "$PWD/$SIPP_LOG"
  docker compose -p "$SIPP_RUN" -f compose.yaml down -v   # remove ONLY the throwaway project + volume
  ```

  `docker compose config` confirms that these variables give project `fsb-sipp-…`, `127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT`, and volume `fsb-sipp-…_postgres_data`. That substitution was checked. The full SIPp run was **not** executed when this skill was generated.

- **Run.** Execute the block above. The script prints a `用例	结果` table, and every row must read `PASS`. Exit code `0` means all cases passed. Any failure appends a `FAIL` row for the current case and exits non-zero.
- **Read the proof.** In `$SIPP_LOG/`: `results.tsv` (PASS/FAIL per case), the observer's INVITE and authorize captures, `containers.log`, `cdr/`, `replay.retry.json` / `replay.archive.json`, and the SQL waits on `raw_cdrs`/`cdrs` keyed by controlled `sip_call_id`. For extra DB proof, query the throwaway DB with `docker compose -p "$SIPP_RUN" -f compose.yaml exec -T postgres psql -U fs_billing -d fs_billing -c "<sql>"` before running `down -v`.
- **Lua unit only.** Run `lua5.3 freeswitch/tests/billing_authorize_test.lua`. Exit `0`.
- **CI evidence.** A PR's `FreeSWITCH integration` workflow uploads the `freeswitch-integration` artifact (the same log dir). `docs/evidence/phase-4-api/ci.txt` records the last all-PASS run.

## Gotchas

- Running `run-integration.sh` with no overrides writes FSI tenants into the user's dev DB and may stop their Postgres container. Always use the throwaway compose project above, and remove it with `down -v` for that project name only.
- `WORKER_SHARED_KEY` is generated per run when unset. Do not export a real key into the logs.
- The time-limit case really waits about 60 s per call, so the whole run takes several minutes. Do not shorten it: billing at 61 s counts as 2 minutes and the assertions are exact.
- If the image for the current `freeswitch/install/**` and `freeswitch/docker/**` hash is not built, CI skips the e2e with a notice. A skipped workflow is not a PASS.
- `launch.sh`'s API and web stack is independent of this. It does not start the Worker, so `/authorize` and CDR ingest cannot be shown through the browser harness.
# Login errors

Login failures must not reveal whether an email exists. An unknown email and a wrong password produce the same status, code, message, and on-screen alert. After 5 failed attempts within 15 minutes, the account is locked for 15 minutes. The page then shows a countdown driven by `retryAfterSeconds` and disables the form, and the DB holds a counter row. An operator's emergency unlock CLI deletes the counter so the user can log in again. A disabled account gets its own message and no session.

## Sub-features

- `same-message`: an unknown email and a wrong password both return `401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS "账号或密码错误"`. The UI shows the alert `登录邮箱或密码错误` / `请检查后重新输入`. Both emails get an `auth.login_failures` row with `failed_count = 1`.
- `clear-abc001`: a correct login deletes that email's counter. The unknown email's counter is removed with `auth:unlock`.
- `lockout`: attempts 1–4 return `401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS` and attempt 5 returns `429 ACCOUNT_LOCKED` with `retryAfterSeconds ≈ 900`. The alert reads `登录失败次数过多，已暂时锁定` / `请在 15分00秒 后重试`. The submit button is disabled and named by the countdown, which ticks down. The email field is disabled. The DB row has `failed_count = 5` and `locked_until ≈ now + 15 min`.
- `locked-correct-password`: the correct password while locked still returns `429 ACCOUNT_LOCKED`.
- `emergency-unlock`: `auth:unlock` prints `解除锁定计数 1 行` and the counter row is gone. A login with the correct password then reaches `/`.
- `disabled`: CDT018's portal login returns `403 ACCOUNT_DISABLED`. The alert reads `该账号已停用` / `如需恢复使用，请联系客服`, and `cdt018` has 0 `auth.session` rows.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- Submit the form on `/login` or `/admin/login` with a wrong password or an unknown email. Both pages share the logic. Admin copy differs only for disabled (`该管理员账号已停用`).
- Repeat wrong passwords five times on one email.
- An operator runs `pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock <email>`. In this skill that is always `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock <email>`.
- Log in as an account whose tenant or profile is disabled.

## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy.
- `hzx014@example.com` has no `auth.login_failures` row. Check with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT * FROM auth.login_failures WHERE email_key='hzx014@example.com'"`. The journey exits 2 if the row exists.
- `abc001@example.com` is not locked. CDT018 is seeded `disabled`.
- Fewer than about 15 credential requests from this machine in the last minute (shared limit of 30/min/IP).

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs login-errors`. Exit `0`, all six steps PASS.
- **Unknown email vs wrong password.** On `/login`, submit `nobody-verify@example.com` / `wrong-password-verify`, then `abc001@example.com` / `wrong-password-verify`. Step `same-message`. Both responses are `401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS` with identical `message`, and `getByRole('alert')` has text `登录邮箱或密码错误请检查后重新输入` both times. Evidence: `01-unknown-email.png`, `02-wrong-password.png`, `browser-http.txt`. `03-counters-after-one-failure.sql.txt` shows both emails with `failed_count 1` and no lock, and the step fails unless both rows exist with exactly those values.
- **Clear counters.** Log in correctly as `abc001`, then run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock nobody-verify@example.com`. Step `clear-abc001`. The `abc001` counter count is `0`. Evidence: `04-unlock-unknown.cli.txt`.
- **Lock the account.** In a fresh page, submit `hzx014@example.com` with `wrong-password-1` through `wrong-password-5`. Step `lockout`. Attempts 1–4 return 401, attempt 5 returns 429 with `retryAfterSeconds` > 850. The alert contains `登录失败次数过多，已暂时锁定` and matches `请在 1[45]分\d{2}秒 后重试`. `button /^请在 \d+分\d{2}秒 后重试$/` is disabled and its name changes after 2 s (for example `15分00秒` to `14分58秒`). `登录邮箱` is disabled. Evidence: `05-locked.png`, `05-locked.aria.yml`. `06-lock-counter.sql.txt` shows `hzx014@example.com | 5 | t`.
- **Correct password while locked.** Run `$S/api.sh login hzx014@example.com locked`. Step `locked-correct-password`. The response is `429 ACCOUNT_LOCKED`. Evidence: `07-locked-correct-password.http`.
- **Emergency unlock.** Run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock hzx014@example.com`. Step `emergency-unlock`. Output `解除锁定计数 1 行` (`08-auth-unlock.cli.txt`). `09-counter-after-unlock.sql.txt` shows `counter_rows 0`. After a reload and a correct login, the URL is `/` and the page shows `HZX014 · 杭州星讯通信有限公司` (`10-after-unlock.png`).
- **Disabled account.** On `/login`, submit `cdt018@example.com` with the seeded password. Step `disabled`. The response is `403 ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, and the alert contains `该账号已停用` and `如需恢复使用，请联系客服` (`11-disabled.png`). `12-disabled-state.sql.txt` shows `CDT018 | disabled | 0` sessions.

## Gotchas

- The lock engages on the fifth failure itself (429 on attempt 5), not on a sixth try. A loop that expects five 401s fails.
- The UI countdown is computed in the browser from `retryAfterSeconds` at response time. Assert that it changes, not an exact second, because the label can read `15分00秒` or `14分59秒`.
- The `auth:unlock` CLI loads the root `.env`. It reaches our container only because `with-env.sh` exports `DATABASE_URL` first, and exported variables win over `.env`. Running it bare unlocks the user's dev DB instead.
- Unknown emails also get counter rows: the API reserves before checking. Clean them up with `auth:unlock` so later runs start clean, as the journey does.
- The API's 30/min/IP credential limit returns `429 RATE_LIMITED` with the alert `请求过于频繁`. That is a different alert from the lock. If you see it, wait a minute and rerun. Do not count it as lockout proof.
- Stale counters expire after 15 minutes through a 1-minute background cleanup. A rerun soon after a failed run may be BLOCKED until you unlock.
- No admin profile is seeded as disabled, so the admin copy `该管理员账号已停用` cannot be shown without changing data. The journey covers the portal copy only. Report the admin variant as not covered.
# Logout

A signed-in user logs out from the account menu: the avatar dropdown on desktop, or the `账号菜单` bottom sheet on mobile. The server deletes that session row and clears the cookie, and the app returns to the zone's login page. Revisiting a protected page afterwards redirects to login, and the old token no longer authenticates.

## Sub-features

- `desktop-menu`: `button '账号菜单'` opens a `menu` showing the account. Clicking `menuitem '退出登录'` returns to `/login`. The `auth.session` row for this browser's token goes from 1 to 0, and the cookie is gone.
- `revisit`: `/` redirects to `/login`, and replaying the old token on `GET /api/auth/session` returns `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`.
- `mobile-sheet`: at 375px, `button '账号菜单'` opens `dialog '账号菜单'`. Clicking `button '退出登录'` returns to `/login`, and live `abc001` sessions drop by exactly 1.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- Desktop (≥ 768px): click the account name (top right), then `退出登录`.
- Mobile (< 768px): tap the avatar (top right), then `退出登录` in the bottom sheet.
- The blocked page (disabled account mid-session) has its own `退出登录` button, which uses the same flow.

## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001@example.com` is not locked.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs logout`. Exit `0`, three steps PASS.
- **Desktop logout.** Log in as `abc001`. The journey reads this browser's session cookie and confirms its session row exists (matched on `sha256(token)`, so the token never appears in a command line). Replaying the complete signed cookie outside the browser on `GET /api/auth/session` returns `200` for `abc001@example.com`. Click `button '账号菜单'`. `menu` contains `abc001@example.com` (`01-account-menu.png`, `.aria.yml`). Click `menuitem '退出登录'`. The URL becomes `/login` and the same query returns `0`. `02-session-row.sql.txt` records the before and after; the token itself is not written. No `better-auth.session_token` cookie remains (`03-after-logout.png`). `browser-http.txt` shows `POST /api/auth/logout -> 204`.
- **Revisit.** Open `/`. The URL becomes `/login`. Replay the same complete signed cookie on `GET /api/auth/session`. The response is now `401 {"code":"UNAUTHENTICATED"}`. Both replays are logged in `browser-http.txt`, so the pair proves the server revoked a cookie that was valid a moment earlier.
- **Mobile logout.** In a 375×812 touch context, log in as `abc001`. Count live sessions with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT count(*) FROM auth.session s JOIN auth.\"user\" u ON u.id=s.user_id WHERE u.email='abc001@example.com' AND s.expires_at > now()"`. Tap `button '账号菜单'`, then `button '退出登录'` inside `dialog '账号菜单'`. The URL becomes `/login` and the count drops by 1. `05-mobile-sessions.sql.txt` records the query and both counts (`04-mobile-after-logout.png`).
- **API view.** Run `$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com l`, then `$S/api.sh POST /api/auth/logout l` returns `204` with `set-cookie: better-auth.session_token=; … Max-Age=0`. Running `$S/api.sh POST /api/auth/logout l` again returns `401`.

## Gotchas

- A replay must send the whole cookie value, `<token>.<signature>`. The API rejects an unsigned token even before logout, so replaying only the token proves nothing.
- Logout deletes only this browser's session. Other sessions for the same user (other journeys, other contexts) stay live. Assert on this token, or on the count dropping by exactly one, never on "zero sessions for the user".
- On desktop the menu item is a Radix `menuitem` inside `menu`. On mobile it is a plain `button` inside `dialog '账号菜单'`. The same selector does not work for both.
- If the logout request fails for a reason other than 401, the UI stays signed in and shows `退出失败，请检查网络后重试`. If you see that, check API health before you retry.
- Logout is excluded from the credential rate limit. It does not use up the 30/min budget.
# Not found (404)

Unknown URLs show a 404 page: the text `404`, `找不到这个页面`, an explanation, and a `返回首页` link to the zone's home. The zone comes from the path: `/admin/...` is the admin zone, everything else is the portal. Signed-out users, or users from the other zone, see a bare 404. Signed-in users in their own zone see it inside the shell. Unknown `/api/*` paths return a JSON `404 NOT_FOUND`.

## Sub-features

- `portal-signed-out`: `/no-such-page` shows `heading '找不到这个页面'` and `页面不存在或链接已失效，请检查地址是否正确`. `link '返回首页'` leads to `/` and then redirects to `/login`.
- `admin-signed-out`: `/admin/no-such-page` shows the admin-zone 404 (`html[data-product=admin]`). `返回首页` leads to `/admin` and then redirects to `/admin/login`.
- `admin-in-shell`: a signed-in admin on `/admin/no-such-page` sees the 404 inside the shell (`button '账号菜单'`, crumb `找不到页面`) with `返回首页即回到「概览」`. `返回首页` leads to `/admin`.
- `portal-in-shell`: a signed-in customer on `/x/y` sees the 404 inside the portal shell, with the crumb `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司`.
- `api-404`: `GET /api/no-such-route` returns `404 {"error":{"code":"NOT_FOUND",…}}`.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- Type or follow a bad link under the portal (`/anything`) or the admin area (`/admin/anything`), signed in or out.
- An HTTP client calls a non-existent `/api/...` path.

## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001` and `wangmin` are not locked.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs not-found`. Exit `0`, five steps PASS.
- **Portal, signed out.** Open `/no-such-page`. The page shows `heading '找不到这个页面'` and the explanation (`01-portal-404.png`, `.aria.yml`). Click `link '返回首页'`. The URL becomes `/login`.
- **Admin, signed out.** Open `/admin/no-such-page`. The page shows the heading and `html[data-product=admin]` (`02-admin-404-signed-out.png`). Click `返回首页`. The URL becomes `/admin/login`.
- **Admin, in shell.** Log in as `wangmin` on `/admin/login`, then open `/admin/no-such-page`. The page shows the heading, `button '账号菜单'`, and `返回首页即回到「概览」` (`03-admin-404-shell.png`, `.aria.yml`). Click `返回首页`. The URL becomes `/admin`.
- **Portal, in shell.** Log in as `abc001`, then open `/x/y`. The page shows the heading and the crumb `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司` (`04-portal-404-shell.png`).
- **API 404.** Run `$S/api.sh GET /api/no-such-route`. The response is `404` with `"code":"NOT_FOUND"` (`05-api-404.http`).

## Gotchas

- The bare 404 and the in-shell 404 depend on whether the session's identity matches the path's zone. A customer on `/admin/nope` gets the bare admin 404, not the portal shell.
- The page renders nothing while the session query is pending. Wait for the heading, not for `load`.
- `vite preview` serves `index.html` for every unknown path (SPA fallback), so the HTTP status of a page 404 is 200. Only `/api/*` gives a real 404 status. Do not assert a 404 status on page URLs.
- `返回首页即回到「概览」` is hidden below 768px. Use a desktop viewport for `admin-in-shell`.
# Session expiry and return-to

When a session expires or is revoked, the user is sent to the login page of their zone. After logging in again, they return to the exact page they were on, query string included. There are two cases. Navigating with an expired session redirects to `/login?reason=expired`, which shows a `登录已过期` banner. A revoked session discovered while on a page opens a blocking dialog, whose `重新登录` button leads to `/admin/login?reason=expired`. A remembered path from the other zone is ignored after login, and the user lands on their own home.

## Sub-features

- `expired-redirect`: with an expired `auth.session.expires_at`, opening `/?tab=ledger&type=%E5%85%85%E5%80%BC` leads to `/login?reason=expired` with the status banner `登录已过期，请重新登录` / `登录后将返回之前的页面`. Logging in returns to the same URL.
- `revoked-dialog`: deleting the admin's session rows while they are on `/admin?view=verify`, then a refocus refetch, opens `dialog '登录已过期，请重新登录'` containing `登录后将回到当前页面`. Clicking `重新登录` goes to `/admin/login?reason=expired`. Logging in returns to `/admin?view=verify`.
- `cross-zone-fallback`: a signed-out visit to `/admin?from=portal-user`, then a customer login on `/admin/login`, lands on `/`.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- Leave a tab past the 7-day session lifetime, then navigate or reload.
- An admin resets your password, or `auth:bootstrap --replace` runs, while you have the app open. Either deletes your sessions. Return to the tab.
- Follow a deep link (bookmark or shared URL) while signed out.

## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001@example.com` and `lijun@example.com` are not locked.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs session-expiry`. Exit `0`, three steps PASS.
- **Expire and deep-link.** Log in as `abc001`. Expire the session in the DB, standing in for 7 days passing: `$S/sql.sh "UPDATE auth.session SET expires_at = now() - interval '1 minute' WHERE user_id = (SELECT id FROM auth.\"user\" WHERE email='abc001@example.com')"` (saved as `01-expire-sessions.sql.txt`). Open `/?tab=ledger&type=%E5%85%85%E5%80%BC`. The URL becomes `/login?reason=expired` and `getByRole('status')` contains both banner lines (`02-expired-banner.png`, `.aria.yml`). Log in again. The URL is exactly `/?tab=ledger&type=%E5%85%85%E5%80%BC` and `heading '首页'` is visible (`03-returned.png`).
- **Revoke while on a page.** Log in on `/admin/login` as `lijun@example.com` and open `/admin?view=verify`. Delete the sessions, as an admin reset does: `$S/sql.sh "DELETE FROM auth.session WHERE user_id = (SELECT id FROM auth.\"user\" WHERE email='lijun@example.com')"` (saved as `04-revoke-sessions.sql.txt`). Dispatch `visibilitychange` on `document`. `dialog '登录已过期，请重新登录'` appears with `登录后将回到当前页面` (`05-expired-dialog.png`, `.aria.yml`). Click `button '重新登录'`. The URL is `/admin/login?reason=expired`. Log in. The URL is `/admin?view=verify` (`06-returned.png`).
- **Wrong-zone return path.** In a fresh context, open `/admin?from=portal-user`. The URL becomes `/admin/login`. Log in as `abc001`. The URL is `/`.
- **API view.** After the expiry update, `$S/api.sh GET /api/auth/session <jar>` returns `401 SESSION_EXPIRED` with `"action":"reauthenticate"`. After the delete, it returns `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`.

## Gotchas

- The two cases differ by server code. `SESSION_EXPIRED` (row exists, past `expires_at`) adds `?reason=expired` and the banner. `UNAUTHENTICATED` (row gone, or cookie cleared) redirects to plain `/login` with no banner. Clearing browser cookies does not prove the expired banner.
- The dialog appears only when an in-page query refetches. Headless Chromium cannot really background a tab, so the journey dispatches `visibilitychange`, which is React Query's refocus trigger. This is the one synthetic event in the skill. Say so when you report.
- The expiry and revocation are DB edits that stand in for time passing and for an admin action. Each one is saved as its own `.sql.txt`. Do not treat them as product behavior.
- The return path lives in `sessionStorage`, so it is per tab. A login in a new tab or context goes home instead.
- Session lifetime is 7 days with no sliding refresh visible to the user. Do not wait for a real expiry.
# Theme and mobile layout

Users pick a light, dark, or follow-system theme. The choice applies at once, persists in `localStorage` across reloads, and in system mode tracks the OS color scheme live. On a 375px phone, the shell switches to a mobile header with a `☰` navigation drawer and an avatar that opens a bottom sheet. The sheet shows the account, a segmented theme picker, and logout, and the content fits without horizontal scrolling.

## Sub-features

- `desktop-dark`: `button '主题'` (◐) opens `menuitemradio '深色'`. Choosing it sets `html[data-theme=dark]` and `localStorage['fs-billing.theme'] = 'dark'`, and the theme is still dark after a reload.
- `desktop-light`: `menuitemradio '浅色'` sets `light` and stores `light`.
- `system`: `menuitemradio '跟随系统'` stores `system`, and `data-theme` follows `prefers-color-scheme` dark and light live, without a reload.
- `mobile-layout`: at 375×812 the portal home shows `region '当前余额'` with `-¥35.20` and `button '打开导航'`, and `scrollWidth` ≤ 375.
- `account-sheet`: `button '账号菜单'` opens `dialog '账号菜单'` with `abc001@example.com`, `深圳市某某科技有限公司 · ABC001`, and `radiogroup '主题'`. Choosing `深色` makes `radio '深色'` checked and the theme dark.
- `nav-drawer`: `button '打开导航'` opens `dialog '导航'`, whose `link '首页'` has `aria-current=page`.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- Desktop, signed in: the ◐ button in the top bar.
- Mobile, signed in: avatar, then the `主题` segmented control in the account sheet.
- The OS appearance setting, when the preference is `跟随系统` (the default, with nothing stored).
- Any page at a narrow viewport (phone, or a narrow window under 768px).

## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001@example.com` is not locked.
- The browser context starts with `colorScheme: 'light'` and empty `localStorage`. Each journey context is new.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs theme-and-mobile`. Exit `0`, six steps PASS.
- **Dark and persistence.** Log in as `abc001`. Click `button '主题'`; `menu` shows three radios (`01-theme-menu.aria.yml`). Click `menuitemradio '深色'`. The page has `html[data-theme=dark]` and storage `dark`. Reload: the theme is still `dark` (`02-dark-after-reload.png`).
- **Light.** Use `主题`, then `浅色`. The page has `data-theme=light` and storage `light`.
- **System.** Use `主题`, then `跟随系统`. Storage is `system`. `page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: 'dark' })` sets `data-theme=dark`, and `'light'` sets `light`, with no reload.
- **Mobile layout.** In a new 375×812 `isMobile` context, log in. The URL is `/`. The page shows `region '当前余额'` with `-¥35.20` and `button '打开导航'`, and `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` ≤ 375 (`03-mobile-home.png`, `.aria.yml`).
- **Account sheet.** Tap `button '账号菜单'`. `dialog '账号菜单'` shows the email, the tenant line, and `radiogroup '主题'` (`04-account-sheet.aria.yml`). Tap `深色`. The page has `data-theme=dark` and `radio '深色'` checked (`04-account-sheet-dark.png`). Press Escape.
- **Nav drawer.** Tap `button '打开导航'`. `dialog '导航'` has `link '首页'` with `aria-current="page"` (`05-nav-drawer.png`).

## Gotchas

- The theme is applied before React renders, by an inline script in `index.html`, using the same `fs-billing.theme` key. A bad stored value falls back to `system`. Assert `data-theme`, not CSS colors.
- The segmented control uses visually hidden native radios. Click the label text (`getByText('深色')` inside the radiogroup) and assert `getByRole('radio', { name: '深色' })` is checked.
- The breakpoint is `md` (768px). At 640–767px the login page is in desktop layout but the shell is mobile. Use exactly 375px for "mobile" proof.
- The theme preference is per browser (localStorage), not per account. A new context resets it, so persistence must be shown with a reload in the same context.
- The theme menu exists only in the signed-in shell. The login pages have no theme control; they follow the stored or system theme.

+    return 1
+  else

@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Self-test for verify_redact in lib.sh. Needs no running stack. Exit 0 when every case
+# redacts as expected, 1 otherwise. Prints case names and diffs, never a real secret
+# (all values below are synthetic).
+# Usage: selftest-redact.sh
+source "$(dirname "$0")/lib.sh"
+
+export SEED_AUTH_PASSWORD='seed-Pw.1' BETTER_AUTH_SECRET='auth-secret-xyz'
+export PG_OWNER_PASSWORD='' PG_APP_PASSWORD='' PG_AUTH_PASSWORD=''
+
+failures=0
+# check <name> <input> <expected output> [leaked value that must not survive]...
+check() {
+  local name=$1 input=$2 expected=$3 actual leak
+  shift 3
+  actual=$(printf '%s' "$input" | verify_redact)
+  for leak in "$@"; do
+    if [[ $actual == *"$leak"* ]]; then
+      echo "FAIL $name: synthetic value '$leak' survived"
+      failures=$((failures + 1))
+      return
+    fi
+  done
+  if [[ $actual != "$expected" ]]; then
+    echo "FAIL $name"
+    diff <(printf '%s\n' "$expected") <(printf '%s\n' "$actual") | sed 's/^/  /'
+    failures=$((failures + 1))
+    return
+  fi
+  echo "ok   $name"
+}
+
+check 'single-line field' \
+  '{"email":"a@example.com","newPassword":"hunter2-x"}' \
+  '{"email":"a@example.com","newPassword":"[REDACTED]"}' \
+  hunter2-x
+
+check 'value on the next line' \
+  $'{\n  "newPassword":\n    "split-value-1"\n}' \
+  $'{\n  "newPassword":\n    "[REDACTED]"\n}' \
+  split-value-1
+
+check 'colon on its own line' \
+  $'{"temporaryPassword"\n:\n"split-value-2"}' \
+  $'{"temporaryPassword"\n:\n"[REDACTED]"}' \
+  split-value-2
+
+check 'pretty-printed, several fields' \
+  $'{\n  "currentPassword": "old-pw-3",\n  "newPassword": "new-pw-3",\n  "email": "keep@example.com"\n}' \
+  $'{\n  "currentPassword": "[REDACTED]",\n  "newPassword": "[REDACTED]",\n  "email": "keep@example.com"\n}' \
+  old-pw-3 new-pw-3
+
+check 'escaped quotes in the value' \
+  '{"password":"a\"b\\\"c-tail","next":"kept"}' \
+  '{"password":"[REDACTED]","next":"kept"}' \
+  'c-tail'
+
+check 'escaped quotes, multi-line' \
+  $'{\n  "token":\n  "x\\"y\\\\\\"z-tail"\n}' \
+  $'{\n  "token":\n  "[REDACTED]"\n}' \
+  'z-tail'
+
+check 'session cookie' \
+  $'set-cookie: better-auth.session_token=abc.def%2F; Path=/\nnext line' \
+  $'set-cookie: better-auth.session_token=[REDACTED]; Path=/\nnext line' \
+  abc.def
+
+check 'run.env secrets anywhere' \
+  $'seed-Pw.1 and\nauth-secret-xyz' \
+  $'[REDACTED] and\n[REDACTED]' \
+  seed-Pw.1 auth-secret-xyz
+
+check 'non-sensitive fields unchanged' \
+  $'{\n  "email": "a@example.com",\n  "passwordChangedAt": "2026-10-14"\n}' \
+  $'{\n  "email": "a@example.com",\n  "passwordChangedAt": "2026-10-14"\n}'
+
+if (( failures )); then
+  echo "selftest-redact: $failures failure(s)" >&2
+  exit 1
+fi
+echo 'selftest-redact: all cases pass'
# FreeSWITCH / SIPp e2e (separate surface)

The telephony surface has no UI. A softswitch user places a call through FreeSWITCH. The billing Lua asks the Worker to authorize it (`maxSeconds`), FreeSWITCH bridges or rejects the call (402/403/484), and on hangup posts the CDR through the nginx relay to the Worker, which inserts `raw_cdrs`/`cdrs` and debits the ledger. `freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh` drives this end to end with real FreeSWITCH, SIPp, nginx, and Worker processes and an observer that captures INVITEs. The browser harness (`drive.mjs`) does not drive it, and `launch.sh` does not start it.

## Sub-features

- `number-cases`: six dialing formats (`+44`, `0044`, domestic mobile, domestic landline, `+1305`, bare `1305`). In each, the upstream INVITE keeps the original number. The authorize request has `callee == billingNumber` normalized and `dialedNumber` raw. The CDR `billing_number` is normalized.
- `rejects`: a disabled tenant gets SIP 403, insufficient balance gets 402, and an invalid number gets 484 before any authorize request. None is bridged upstream.
- `degrade`: when the Worker refuses or stalls, the call degrades per the Lua rules, and the observer records no authorize request.
- `time-limit`: a real 60 s allotted call lasts 59–60 s. It is billed 1 minute, CDR amount `0.01`, ledger `-0.01`, balance `0.01 → 0`.
- `cdr-replay`: with nginx down, the CDR lands in the retry dir. `replay-cdr.sh` re-posts it after nginx returns, and exactly one charge results.
- `lua-unit`: `lua5.3 freeswitch/tests/billing_authorize_test.lua` (also run by `pnpm check`).

## How to get to it (user POV)

- CI: `.github/workflows/freeswitch.yml` job `e2e`, on push or PR touching `freeswitch/**`, `apps/worker/**`, or `packages/**`, nightly, or `workflow_dispatch`. It needs the private GHCR image built by the `image` job (`SIGNALWIRE_TOKEN`).
- Locally: `bash freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh [log-dir]`, with the images `fs-billing-freeswitch:local` and the SIPp image built from `freeswitch/test/sipp.Dockerfile`. `--build` rebuilds FreeSWITCH and needs `SIGNALWIRE_TOKEN`.
- A real test-bench call (phase 10) is a different acceptance, outside this skill.

## Driving it with run-integration.sh

Preconditions:

- Linux with Docker host networking, Node, pnpm, Python 3, curl, and `docker images` showing `fs-billing-freeswitch:local` (or set `FS_IMAGE`).
- Host ports `3001` (Worker), `3002` (nginx relay), `3003` (stall), `3004` (authorize observer), and `5060`/`5062` (SIP) are free. They are fixed and host-networked, so only one SIPp run per machine, and never alongside a dev Worker on 3001.
- **Isolation.** By default the script runs `docker compose -f compose.yaml` against the user's compose project, which is `fs-billing-postgres-1` on `:5432`, migrates it, inserts `FSI…` test tenants, and stops it afterwards if it started it. Never run it bare here. Point it at a throwaway compose project instead:

  ```sh
  SIPP_RUN=fsb-sipp-$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)
  SIPP_PORT=$(python3 -c 'import socket; s=socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1",0)); print(s.getsockname()[1])')
  SIPP_PW=$(python3 -c 'import secrets; print(secrets.token_hex(16))')
  SIPP_LOG=docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/$SIPP_RUN/freeswitch-sipp
  COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=$SIPP_RUN POSTGRES_PORT=$SIPP_PORT POSTGRES_PASSWORD=$SIPP_PW \
  DATABASE_URL=postgresql://fs_billing:$SIPP_PW@127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT/fs_billing \
  APP_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://fs_billing_app:$SIPP_PW-app@127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT/fs_billing \
    bash freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh "$PWD/$SIPP_LOG"
  docker compose -p "$SIPP_RUN" -f compose.yaml down -v   # remove ONLY the throwaway project + volume
  ```

  `docker compose config` confirms that these variables give project `fsb-sipp-…`, `127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT`, and volume `fsb-sipp-…_postgres_data`. That substitution was checked. The full SIPp run was **not** executed when this skill was generated.

- **Run.** Execute the block above. The script prints a `用例	结果` table, and every row must read `PASS`. Exit code `0` means all cases passed. Any failure appends a `FAIL` row for the current case and exits non-zero.
- **Read the proof.** In `$SIPP_LOG/`: `results.tsv` (PASS/FAIL per case), the observer's INVITE and authorize captures, `containers.log`, `cdr/`, `replay.retry.json` / `replay.archive.json`, and the SQL waits on `raw_cdrs`/`cdrs` keyed by controlled `sip_call_id`. For extra DB proof, query the throwaway DB with `docker compose -p "$SIPP_RUN" -f compose.yaml exec -T postgres psql -U fs_billing -d fs_billing -c "<sql>"` before running `down -v`.
- **Lua unit only.** Run `lua5.3 freeswitch/tests/billing_authorize_test.lua`. Exit `0`.
- **CI evidence.** A PR's `FreeSWITCH integration` workflow uploads the `freeswitch-integration` artifact (the same log dir). `docs/evidence/phase-4-api/ci.txt` records the last all-PASS run.

## Gotchas

- Running `run-integration.sh` with no overrides writes FSI tenants into the user's dev DB and may stop their Postgres container. Always use the throwaway compose project above, and remove it with `down -v` for that project name only.
- `WORKER_SHARED_KEY` is generated per run when unset. Do not export a real key into the logs.
- The time-limit case really waits about 60 s per call, so the whole run takes several minutes. Do not shorten it: billing at 61 s counts as 2 minutes and the assertions are exact.
- If the image for the current `freeswitch/install/**` and `freeswitch/docker/**` hash is not built, CI skips the e2e with a notice. A skipped workflow is not a PASS.
- `launch.sh`'s API and web stack is independent of this. It does not start the Worker, so `/authorize` and CDR ingest cannot be shown through the browser harness.
# Admin login

An internal admin signs in on `/admin/login` and lands in the admin shell at `/admin`: the `概览` page, the admin's name in the account menu, and the admin accent (`html[data-product=admin]`). Where you land depends on the account kind in the login response, not on the login page used. A customer who signs in on the admin login page lands in the portal, and an admin who signs in on the portal login page lands in the admin shell.

## Sub-features

- `entry-redirect`: a signed-out visit to `/admin` redirects to `/admin/login`, which shows `heading '登录管理后台'` and the hint `客户请使用客户门户登录`.
- `admin-shell`: `wangmin@example.com` reaches `/admin` with `heading '概览'`, `button '账号菜单'` containing `王敏`, and the text `欢迎，王敏。…`. An `auth.session` row joined to `admins` exists.
- `kind-decides-zone`: a customer logging in on `/admin/login` lands on `/`, and a later visit to `/admin` bounces to `/`.
- `admin-via-portal-login`: an admin logging in on `/login` lands on `/admin`.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- Open `/admin/login` directly.
- Open any `/admin…` URL while signed out. The app redirects to `/admin/login`.
- Log in as an admin on the portal page `/login`. The app routes by account kind.

## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy.
- `wangmin@example.com` and `abc001@example.com` are not locked.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs admin-login`. Exit `0`, and `steps.md` lists the four sub-feature IDs as PASS.
- **Signed-out redirect.** Open `/admin`. The URL becomes `/admin/login`, and the page shows `heading '登录管理后台'` and `客户请使用客户门户登录`. Evidence: `01-admin-login.png`, `.aria.yml`.
- **Admin login.** On `/admin/login`, fill `登录邮箱` with `wangmin@example.com` and the seeded password, then click `登录`. The URL becomes `/admin`, and the page shows `heading '概览'`, `欢迎，王敏。`, `button '账号菜单'` with `王敏`, and `html[data-product=admin]`. Evidence: `02-admin-home.png`, `.aria.yml`. `02-admin-session.sql.txt` shows `wangmin@example.com | 王敏 | t`.
- **Customer on the admin page.** In a fresh context, log in on `/admin/login` as `abc001@example.com`. The URL becomes `/` and `heading '首页'` is visible. Then `page.goto('/admin')` stays on `/`. Evidence: `03-customer-via-admin-login.png`.
- **Admin on the portal page.** In a fresh context, log in on `/login` as `wangmin@example.com`. The URL becomes `/admin`.
- **API view.** Run `$S/api.sh login wangmin@example.com adm` then `$S/api.sh GET /api/admin/context adm`. The response is 200 with `"identity":{"kind":"admin",…,"name":"王敏"}`.

## Gotchas

- `/admin/login` shows `登录管理后台` as the h1 on both desktop and mobile. The mobile layout also shows an `FS-Billing 管理后台` pill. Do not assert the portal copy here.
- Routing by kind happens client-side after a 200 login. The API accepts a customer login on either page, so the API response alone never proves the zone. Assert the final URL.
- Admin login creates a normal 7-day session, and nothing in this journey logs it out. Later journeys that count `wangmin` sessions must count before and after rather than expect zero.
# Cross-tenant isolation

A customer can read only their own tenant's data, and customer and admin APIs are kept apart. The portal API takes no tenant or account ID from the client: the tenant comes from the session. So customer A's cookie always returns A's data, and B's IDs in admin URLs are refused with 403. A customer cookie on admin endpoints and an admin cookie on portal endpoints both get `403 FORBIDDEN`, and no cookie gets `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`. The repo's IDOR suite enumerates every customer, authenticated, and session operation from the live OpenAPI and checks both defenses: explicit filters and RLS.

## Sub-features

- `own-tenant-only`: A (`abc001`) and B (`glb009`) each `GET /api/portal/context` and each body equals, field for field, the only context that customer may see: its own profile, email, tenant (id, code, name, currency from the DB) and balance (A `-35.20 CNY`, B `-15.02 USD`). Any extra field fails the step.
- `customer-to-admin`: A's cookie on `GET /api/admin/context` and on `POST /api/admin/portal-accounts/<B's id>/reset-password` gets `403 FORBIDDEN`. B's `auth.account.updated_at` is unchanged and `operation_logs` has 0 `reset_password` rows.
- `admin-to-portal`: an admin cookie on `GET /api/portal/context` gets `403 FORBIDDEN`.
- `anonymous`: no cookie gets `401 UNAUTHENTICATED` with `action: reauthenticate`.
- `idor-suite`: `pnpm test:idor` reports `Tests 15 passed (15)`, run on a scratch `fs_billing_test` DB inside the run's container.

## How to get to it (user POV)

- A logged-in customer's browser calls `/api/portal/context` from the portal home. Any HTTP client holding their cookie can call any `/api/*` path.
- A customer pastes an admin URL such as `/admin` into the browser. The UI bounces them to `/` (see [admin-login](./admin-login.md)), and this feature proves the API also refuses.
- A developer runs `pnpm test:idor`, and CI runs it too.

## Driving it with curl via api.sh (drive.mjs orchestrates)

Preconditions:

- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001`, `glb009`, and `wangmin` are not locked.
- `operation_logs` has no `reset_password` rows. A fresh launch has none.

- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs cross-tenant`. Exit `0`, five steps PASS. Transcripts are saved as `NN-*.http`.
- **Two tenants.** Run `$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com a`, `$S/api.sh login glb009@example.com b`, `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context a`, and `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context b`. Jar `a` returns `"code":"ABC001"` and `"amount":"-35.20"`. Jar `b` returns `"code":"GLB009"` and `"amount":"-15.02"`. The journey compares each whole body with one built from the DB for that customer, so an extra key or foreign tenant data fails. `05-tenant-ids.sql.txt` maps codes to ids and balances.
- **Customer on admin API.** Run `$S/api.sh GET /api/admin/context a`. The response is `403` with `"code":"FORBIDDEN"`. Get B's portal account id with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT p.id FROM portal_accounts p JOIN tenants t ON t.id=p.tenant_id WHERE t.code='GLB009'"`, then run `$S/api.sh POST /api/admin/portal-accounts/<id>/reset-password a`. The response is `403 FORBIDDEN`. `08-b-credential-before-after.sql.txt` records B's `auth.account.updated_at` before and after the attempt, and the step fails unless they are equal. `08-no-reset-side-effect.sql.txt` shows the row and `reset_logs 0`.
- **Admin on portal API.** Run `$S/api.sh login wangmin@example.com admin` then `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context admin`. The response is `403 FORBIDDEN`.
- **Anonymous.** Run `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context`. The response is `401` with `"code":"UNAUTHENTICATED"` and `"action":"reauthenticate"`.
- **IDOR suite.** Run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm test:idor`. The output contains `Tests  15 passed (15)` (`13-test-idor.cli.txt`). The suite creates and drops `fs_billing_test` in OUR container. Confirm with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT datname FROM pg_database"`: no `fs_billing_test` remains.
- **Mutation proof (optional, slower).** Run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api prove:idor docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/cross-tenant/prove-idor`. It must report that baseline-app and baseline-owner pass, that mutation-owner detects B's balance leak, and that mutation-app passes (`docs/phase-4-api.md`).

## Gotchas

- There is no portal endpoint that takes a tenant id, so "A reads B by changing an id" cannot be attempted on the portal API. The real attack surfaces are the admin endpoints that take ids (the reset-password routes) and session or cookie confusion. Do not invent a URL like `/api/portal/tenants/<B>`: it returns `404 NOT_FOUND`, which proves nothing.
- `test:idor` and `prove:idor` load the root `.env`. Run them only through `with-env.sh`, or they drop and recreate `fs_billing_test` on the user's `:5432` server.
- Do not run `test:idor` while another DB suite runs against the same container. It drops and recreates `fs_billing_test` every time.
- POSTs need `Origin` set to a trusted origin, or they return `403 FORBIDDEN` before authorization runs. `api.sh` sends the web origin. A hand-written curl without it gives a misleading 403.
